If value moves on-chain, identity must move with it.

CRYMBO is a full-stack infrastructure platform that enables regulated institutions to operate crypto and blockchain-based financial activity safely and at scale.

The Infrastructure for Verified Value Movement

CRYMBO enables banks, EMIs, payment providers, OTC desks, and asset issuers to move fiat, crypto, and real-world assets across blockchains while meeting regulatory requirements by design.

Time to Market

Launch without building an in-house infrastructure stack.

Speed

Real-time validation and settlement.

Risk Reduction

Compliance enforced before execution.

Confidence

Verified identity and deterministic compliance.

Integrate Today

Use blockchain safely by making sure identity and rules travel with every transaction.

CRYMBO is the first and only infrastructure that enables identity to move directly on-chain, alongside the transaction itself, while keeping the activity fully decentralized. This removes the need to rely on fragmented off-chain processes, centralized databases, and manual compliance flows, allowing institutions to scale without operational friction

  Interface layer

CRYMBO Platform: Full-stack infrastructure layer that enables institutions to operate regulated on-chain activity from a single system. It provides customizable dashboards, workflows, and APIs to manage identity, approvals, reporting, and integrations with external providers in an automated way.
‍‍

Identity Layer

CRYMBO Oracle: A decentralized on-chain identity infrastructure that allows verified identity to move alongside transactions, enabling compliant on-chain activity in real time, including regulated Real-World Asset workflows.
‍

Execution Layer

Blockchain: Execute compliant transactions directly on public L1s and L2s - where users and liquidity already exist.

For Regulated Institutions
Launch digital asset products and scale regulated on-chain operations.

CRYMBO provides regulated institutions with a unified infrastructure to operate crypto, payments, and digital assets with compliance built in by design.Through a single platform, CRYMBO enables on / off-ramps, portfolio and treasury management, and payments  with integrated KYC, KYT, and Travel Rule workflows applied directly to transaction flows.

With CRYMBO, institutions can:

  • Launch crypto and payment products in weeks, not months
  • Move money instantly between verified counterparties
  • Reduce reliance on fragmented vendors and manual processes
  • Operate across TradFi, CeFi, and DeFi from one systemScale globally while enforcing local regulatory requirements

CRYMBO is built for financial institutions that require speed, control, and regulatory certainty when operating on-chain.

  • Banks and EMIs embedding crypto and digital asset services
  • Payment providers enabling compliant crypto settlement
  • OTC desks and VASPs scaling transaction volume without scaling compliance overhead
  • ‍Asset issuers launching and managing regulated digital assets
